# original: converted structures

Generated by `tools/convert_blueprints.py`. Build with `/colony build original/<path>`.

## Fixing a building by hand

1. Get a structure block (`/give @s structure_block`), place it, and set it to **Load** mode.
2. Enter the structure name (e.g. `minecp:original/fundamentals/builder1`), press LOAD once to see the outline, again to place it.
3. The structure's corner is the structure block position plus the offset (default `0 1 0`). Positions below are `x y z` from that corner.
4. Replace the approximated blocks listed for that building.
5. Switch the structure block to **Save** mode with the same name and size, and press SAVE.
   It saves to `world/generated/minecp/structure/`, which takes priority over the data pack, so your fix is used and re-running the converter never touches it.
6. Keep the barrier blocks in the ground: they mark foundation filler.

## original/agriculture/fields/bamboo
Size 14x6x11.
- **102x** structurize:blocksolidsubstitution -> minecraft:barrier (foundation filler: the builder puts dirt there only where the ground is not solid)
- **1x** minecolonies:blockhutplantationfield -> minecraft:oak_trapdoor: `8 1 5`
